# Antimonides

Units: T is in K; $Cp^{\circ}$ and $S^{\circ}$ are in cal/mol·K; $H^{\circ} - H_{298}^{\circ}$ , $\Delta Hf^{\circ}$ and $\Delta Gf^{\circ}$ are in kcal/mol.

Sources of data from general references are listed below, with the reference given immediately after each formula:

GaSb(c) (30), InSb(c,1) (30), USb2(c) (33), U3Sb4(c) (33).

Additional sources:

AlSb(c,1):

Samokhval, V. V., and A. A. Vecher. Thermodynamic Properties of Aluminum Antimonide. Russ. J. Phys. Chem. (Engl. Transl.), v. 42, 1968, pp. 340-341.

Sirota, N. N., and V. Z. Golodushko. (Determination of the Dissociation Pressure of Gallium Phosphide and Aluminum Antimonide.) Tezisy Dokl.-Vses. Konf. Khim. Svyazi Poluprovodn. i Polumet., 5th, 1974, p. 98.

AlSb(c,l), GaSb(c,l), InSb(c,l):

Lichter, B. D., and P. Sommelet. Thermal Properties of A $^{III}$ B $^{V}$ Compounds. II. High-Temperature Heat Contents and Heats of Fusion of InSb, GaSb, and AlSb. Trans. Metall. Soc. AIME, v. 245, 1969, pp. 99-105.

Piesbergen, U. (The Average Atomic Heats of the A $^{III}$ B $^{V}$ Semiconductors AlSb, GaAs, GaSb, InP, InAs, InSb and the Atomic Heat of the Element Ge Between 12 and 273°K.) Z. Naturforsch., A, v. 18, 1963, pp. 141-147.

CrSb(c,1):

Alles, A., B. Falk, E. F. Westrum, Jr., and F. Gronvold. Heat Capacity and Thermodynamic Properties of $CrSb_{2}$ from 5 to 1050 K. Magnetic Transition and Enthalpy of Decomposition. J. Chem. Thermodyn., v. 10, 1978, pp. 103-116.

FeSb $_{2}$ (c,1):

Gronvold, F., A. J. Highe, and E. F. Westrum, Jr. Heat Capacity and Thermodynamic Properties of $FeSb_{2}$ from 5 to 1021.2 K. Enthalpy of Decomposition. J. Chem. Thermodyn., v. 9, 1977, pp. 773-782.

USb2(c):

Westrum, E. F., Jr., J. A. Sommers, D. B. Downie, and F. Gronvold. Cryogenic Heat Capacities and Neel Transitions of Two Uranium Pnictides, UAs₂ and USb₂. Thermodynamics of Nuclear Materials 1974. Int. At. Energy Agency, v. 2, 1975, pp. 409-418.

$U_{3}Sb_{4}(c)$ :

Alles, A. Heat Capacities, Thermodynamics, and Magnetic Transitions of Some Uranium and Chromium Pnictides. Ph. D. Thesis, Univ. MI, Ann Arbor, MI, 1975, 119 pp.

# Arsenides

Units: T is in K; $Cp^{\circ}$ and $S^{\circ}$ are in cal/mol·K; $H^{\circ} - H_{298}^{\circ}$ , $\Delta Hf^{\circ}$ and $\Delta Gf^{\circ}$ are in kcal/mol.

Sources of data from general references are listed below, with the reference given immediately after each formula:

$Cd_{3}As_{2}(c)$ (30), InAs(c,1) (30), $U_{3}As_{4}(c)$ (33).

Additional sources:

$\mathrm{Cd}_{3}\mathrm{As}_{2}(\mathrm{c})$

Demidenko, A. F., G. N. Danilenko, V. E. Danilenko, V. B. Lazarev, V. Ya. Shevchenko, S. F. Marenkin, and S. E. Kozlov. Specific Heat and Thermodynamic Properties of Compounds $A_{3}$ III B $_{2}$ V. Inorg. Mater. (Engl. Transl.), v. 13, 1977, pp. 181-183.

GaAs(c,1):

Dash, A. J., A. Finch, P. J. Gardner, and M. Cottrell. Heat Capacity of Gallium Arsenide. J. Chem. and Eng. Data, v. 19, 1974, pp. 113-114.

Lichter, B. D., and P. Sommelet. Thermal Properties of A $^{III}$ B $^{V}$ Compounds: II. High-Temperature Heat Contents and Heats of Fusion of InAs and GaAs. Trans. Metall. Soc. AIME, v. 245, 1969, pp. 99-105.

Panish, M. B. Thermodynamic Evaluation of the Simple Solution Treatment of the Gallium-Phosphorus, Indium-Phosphorus, and Gallium-Arsenide Systems. J. Cryst. Growth, v. 27, 1974, pp. 6-20.

Piesbergen, U. (The Average Atomic Heats of the A $^{III}$ B $^{V}$ Semiconductors AlSb, GaAs, InP, InAs, InSb and the Atomic Heat of the Element Ga Between 12 and 273° K.) Z. Naturforsch., A, v. 18, 1963, pp. 141-147.

Pupp, C., J. J. Murray, and R. F. Pottie. Vapor Pressure of Arsenic Over InAs(c) and GaAs(c). The Enthalpies of Formation of InAs(c) and GaAs(c). J. Chem. Thermodyn., v. 6, 1974, pp. 123-124.

InAs(c,1):

Lichter, B. D., and P. Sommelet. Thermal Properties of A $^{III}$ B $^{V}$ Compounds: II. High-Temperature Heat Contents and Heats of Fusion of InAs and GaAs. Trans. Metall. Soc. AIME, v. 245, 1969, pp. 99-105.

All other data were from reference 1.
